# Package report: ArakelovGeometryAndAbelianHeightsPartII

## Result and input boundary

The package contains a 14-layer reader generated from all 93 accepted-plan nodes, an acceptance-test Lean file, metadata with topic math.NT, and a sources manifest. There are 90 retained target subsections; three exact duplicates are consumed in supplier contracts. Their API requirements and Checks remain in those contracts. Every retained definition/construction has its API list and at least three Checks. The original worked computations are retained, including the banana, loop, theta coefficients, tetrahedron, degree normalizations and the genus-dependent constants.

The input `packages/ArakelovGeometryAndAbelianHeightsPartII/design/review.json` and the pre-existing reports were absent. Acceptance was verified in the embedded top-level review of `design/plan.json`, including its per-node notes. No external document was treated as instructions. Only the top-level gaps value changed in the design plan; every other JSON value was compared with the tracked original and is equal. The README skeleton was generated from the plan before prose conversion.

Shape examples read on main were `TauCetiRoadmap/ComplexTori/README.md`, `TauCetiRoadmap/AnalyticToricGeometry/README.md` and `TauCetiRoadmap/AlgebraicVectorBundles/README.md`; metadata was compared with ComplexTori. The content snapshot is 66576b61d0440aec1f04a10b179f7e7163140b96. Its supplied pins are Mathlib 6b7abb3c7686292736be2955bd3eb9ebf63b456a and Tau Ceti a91d3aafa8cd3e6bc33dfde0d7677ed0f1625039, replacing the older hashes recorded in the read-only design baseline.

## Headline theorem inventory

No headline can be stated faithfully with a few simple definitions on the pinned carriers. The existence of ordinary schemes, abelian varieties and algebraic curve degree does not supply arithmetic metrics or intersections. The acceptance file consequently contains one module docstring, the single Mathlib import and the namespace, with no definitions or theorem stand-ins. The following is the complete inventory considered for that file.

No Lean names are assigned to absent declarations. The general quasi-projective equidistribution theorem and the projective intersection pairing are suppliers, so they are not extra headlines of this package. Bost's theorem is also imported; §13.7 retains its comparison-height application.

## Mathematical corrections and scope clarifications

| Location | Correction | Discriminating computation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| §0.1 | Semipositivity is local positivity of the Chern current/psh weights; the claimed equivalence using only compact-curve tests was dropped. | A norm e on Spec ℤ has curvature 0 but degree −1, separating semipositivity from arithmetic nefness. |
| §0.5 | Split the Siu perturbation into positive and zero mixed-degree cases. | For A=O(2), B=O(0), the mixed degree is 0 and vol(A−εB)=2 for every ε>0. |
| §2.1 | Distinguished scheme seminorm charts with arbitrary coordinate radii from a single bounded affinoid spectrum. | ord₀(t) gives |t|=e⁻¹, while ord∞ gives |t|=e>1. |
| §4.1 | Normalizing a compactification does not preserve a nonnormal U as the same open; pull sections back injectively by dominance instead. | In k[t²,t³]⊂k[t], t lies only in the normalization; pullback does not identify the two coordinate rings. |
| §4.7 | Defined the Moriwaki condition and corrected the geometric constant-height denominator. | Over k(t), a degree-one base line gives constant height 1, with no [K:ℚ] denominator; over a quadratic number field raw degree 2 gives absolute height 1. |
| §5.2 | Rigidified O(e) in the elliptic-family Check. | If e*O(e)=N, then the normalized line has zero pullback N⊗N∨=O; O(e) itself can have N≠O. |
| §§7.3–7.4 | Kept the independently normalized genus-one probability measure separate from the genus>1 canonical pair. | At g=1, deg ω=0 cannot recover a probability measure by division; a theta line of positive degree can. |
| §8.4 | Put multiplication by 2 on the Jacobian factor of J×_S X. | A constant metric twist c=1 gives invariant-weight defect 1−4=−3 on Θ; the curve has no multiplication-by-2 group map. |
| §10.10 | Made the regular triple-product and numerically trivial fibre-extension inputs to the special height explicit. | For hyperelliptic g=2 and φ=5, W=2 gives (5/2)W−φ=0; degree-two extension gives W=4,φ=10 and the same zero. |
| §12.3 | Require m≥max(1,dim S); m=0 is not a potential-bigness witness. | Over a point, the zero exterior sum on the zeroth power is unrelated to the positive degree a=2 of a line on a curve. |
| §12.4 | Spelled out m≥1 and finite positive codimension on a nonempty reduced closed subset; prove finiteness by finite subsets first. | A 3×3 grid has degree 9, codimension 2 and bound √9=3. |
| §12.6 | Restrict to integral tests in characteristic fibres instead of assigning the integral-scheme adelic group to a nonreduced fibre. | ℤ[x]/(x²−2) is flat integral; at 2 its fibre is 𝔽₂[x]/(x²), while the reduced integral test is Spec 𝔽₂. |
| §13.1 | Retained residue degrees over an arbitrary fixed constant base and semistable identity-component base change. | A degree-two field extension doubles geometric h; an auxiliary semistable extension does not change the divided height. |
| §13.5 | Dropped the unverified alternative slope-inequality proof route; retained the graph/Hodge-index route at all characteristics. | A(4)=139<144 and the genus-two improved constant is 429/4=107.25, sufficient for W≥h/12. |
| Source locators | Corrected the Yuan–Zhang pagination assertion and Moriwaki §3.3.6 locator. | The cached v9 PDF has 290 pages; its Theorem 6.1.1 is printed p.180 at PDF p.190, while early pages coincide. |

The accepted corrections also remain in force: Green function −log‖s‖ and metric exp(−g); absolute arithmetic versus relative geometric heights; the factor [K:ℚ] in the fundamental inequality and Hodge degree; geometrically reduced fibres for integral Picard pullback; ramification rather than residue/field degree in local valuation rescaling; the φ integral on the curve square; the coefficient c(g)Δ in the coercive-divisor estimate; and the Faltings-normalized Bost constant −(g/2)log(2π²). The independent computations below reproduce their witnesses. Original review assertions about suggested coefficient lemmas were not followed: those computations remain README Checks, not headline-theorem substitutes.

## Duplication removals and ownership

Ownership was searched by mathematical object in main roadmaps, the supplied open-head index and all board package READMEs. The roadmaps service was used for the completion/Picard/intersection, Deligne, admissible metric/measure, Moriwaki, Gross–Schoen, bigness, boundary, Monge–Ampère and Hilbert–Samuel searches. The pinned source trees were separately searched for the same carriers, metric data, intersection/degree operations and hypotheses.

The native Euler-degree definition is truncated χ through H¹ and requires the stated curve hypotheses for its geometric interpretation. |

The actual open-head ArithmeticDynamics reader, rather than the board copy, supplied the §4.3a/§4.4 ownership verdict. The ordinary projective line metric of that roadmap is not the arbitrary-base relative canonical curve pair. AbelianSchemesAndArithmeticModuliPartII, HeegnerPointEulerSystems, ArithmeticKTheory, GanGrossPrasadUnitaryGroups and the other named open-head matches were inspected as overlap leads; their Hodge/algebraic or specialized arithmetic-cycle applications do not own this relative canonical-bundle bigness theory. GrossZagierAndArithmeticHeightsPartII's board design concerns unitary cycles and their higher Green products, not these admissible curve-family metrics. No declaration in Suggested.lean reuses another roadmap's fully qualified name, since there are no declarations.

The service could not supply GrossZagierAndArithmeticHeights, TropicalAndBerkovichArithmetic or StableReductionPartII from main or an open head. StableReductionPartII's supplied board design is readable and its cited target IDs were verified there. The first two have no readable roadmap body in their supplied package directories. Their historical section labels are therefore exact requested contracts, not a claim that their full contents were verified. Their two explicit gaps preserve ownership without claiming unavailable text. Ownership against the named readable open heads was checked; no unprovided open head was sought independently.

## Supplier gaps and merge direction

The gaps array now contains three exact contracts, each mirrored verbatim under its supplier in the README:

- singular-pluripotential-products: ComplexComparisonPartII §C5.4. Its smooth current calculus and Poincaré–Lelong already exist as roadmap targets; the gap is the singular Bedford–Taylor/regularization extension.
- curve-local-analytic-contract: TropicalAndBerkovichArithmetic TB.0, TB.2, TB.3 and TB.6. Its body was unavailable for exact verification; the requested output and normalization are fully stated.
- archimedean-curve-admissibility: GrossZagierAndArithmeticHeights GZ.2. Its body was unavailable for exact verification; the normalized Green/adjunction and skeleton comparisons are fully stated.
StableReductionPartII MC.6/arbitrary-field-hodge-positivity and MC.6/finite-cover-hodge-descent explicitly supply arbitrary-field and inseparable-cover positivity. The full board design was read through these extensions; an initially suspected gap was discharged and is not recorded as a supplier request.

G3 is represented by the settled uncertified primary Noether locators and the explicit metric-comparison proof target; G4's regular-model source is now legally available and its construction is written in §10.10. G5's sharp asymptotic expansions are outside the accepted end conclusions, and G7's genus-one canonical pair is excluded by explicit g>1 hypotheses; the independent genus-one admissible measure remains. They are not unmet supplier contracts and are no longer entries in the supplier-gap array.

No notion was moved from a later roadmap. All mathematical suppliers are earlier than this package; the downstream uniform-Bogomolov roadmap is not an input. The mechanical dependency output is:

```text
depcheck: 29 roadmaps, 261 edges, 0 problem(s)
```

There is no UPWARD, CYCLE or moved line.

## Source and citation audit

Twenty distinct work keys occur in sources.toml: eighteen certified legal texts and two uncertified published locators. All fetches used the sources service. The author-hosted Yuan manuscript was classified author-page, arXiv and Numdam copies open-repository. No fetched paper text was copied into the package or this report; the short pinned code definitions below are quoted to specify the native carriers. The original Faltings text was not obtained: the service reported unclassified JSTOR/Annals landing pages; its correct published DOI is recorded as uncertified under the brief's locator-only rule. Moret-Bailly was recorded uncertified by the service. Their provisional DOI attempts were corrected to 10.2307/2007043 and 10.1007/BF01393833 under the same work keys. Neither is an outstanding text request. No paywall was bypassed.

The original Noether metric proof remains uncertified; the numerical normalization is independently explicit in Yuan p.89 and Wilms' delta formula. No claim that the original proof was read is made. The Gross–Schoen construction is read in its §§6–8, especially the regular model and fibre-numerically-trivial extension; the number-field height construction there is combined with Zhang's stated global-field formula, rather than extrapolating its number-field proof to every base.

The citation resolver reports no missing Lean names. It cannot see the worker's pinned Lake tree, so every native name was checked locally as described below. Its three roadmap leads are the unavailable main/open bodies just discussed; StableReductionPartII is resolved against the supplied board design and the other two are explicit supplier gaps. Old R35/R11/JC/RP identifiers in the design were replaced by the verified numeric sections where available.

## Elaboration, identifiers and explicit binders

From the content checkout, ran the required lake env lean command on the absolute package-file argument; its repository-relative name is `packages/ArakelovGeometryAndAbelianHeightsPartII/Suggested.lean`. Exit code: 0. Exact sorry-warning count: **0**. Other messages: **none**. No TauCeti module is imported by that file and no build was needed.

The mechanically enumerated declaration set is empty, so the required #check @name comparison for every package declaration is vacuous: there is no section-variable capture and no hypothesis was silently dropped. A separate scratch elaboration checked the two native Mathlib inputs and the two basic Tau Ceti carriers, using the individual Basic modules whose object files were present. It returned:

```text
@NumberField.prod_abs_eq_one : ∀ {K : Type u_1} [inst : Field K] [inst_1 : NumberField K] {x : K},
  x ≠ 0 → (∏ w, w x ^ w.mult) * ∏ᶠ (w : NumberField.FinitePlace K), w x = 1
@Projectivization.logHeight : {K : Type u_1} →
  [inst : Field K] → [Height.AdmissibleAbsValues K] → {ι : Type u_2} → [Finite ι] → Projectivization K (ι → K) → ℝ
TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.InvertibleSheaf : AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme → Type (u_1 + 1)
TauCeti.AlgebraicGeometry.AbelianVariety : (K : Type u_1) → [Field K] → Type (u_1 + 1)
```

The pinned definition at `TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry/LineBundle/Basic.lean:112` is:

```lean
abbrev InvertibleSheaf (X : Scheme.{u}) : Type _ :=
  ObjectProperty.FullSubcategory (SheafOfModules.isInvertible X)
```

The pinned degree body at `TauCeti/AlgebraicGeometry/LineBundle/Degree.lean:70` is:

```lean
def eulerDegree (L : InvertibleSheaf X) : ℤ :=
  Scheme.Modules.eulerCharBelow k X L.obj 2 -
    Scheme.Modules.eulerCharBelow k X (trivial X).obj 2
```

The Euler-degree and canonical-degree declarations were inspected in their pinned source modules, including the namespace and all explicit hypotheses; they are not used by Suggested.lean. InvertibleSheaf is a full subcategory whose morphisms are sheaf-module maps; the definition does not impose invertibility of a morphism, metric compatibility, properness or nonemptiness. AbelianVariety carries a proper geometrically integral group object, not a metric. The product formula has its explicit x≠0 hypothesis, and logHeight keeps admissible absolute values and finite coordinate type. The README was adjusted to those elaborated input types. No test encodings of missing metrics, no free Prop fields, no sorry'd data, no options and no supplementary imports occur in the acceptance file. Missing own Lean identifier count: **0**.

## Adversarial pass: conventions, interfaces and boundary objects

The following computations are probes of the definitions and exact source statements, not proofs by testing. Each target and each API assertion is separately indexed below. Rows sharing a model reuse the displayed calculation; the defining laws and their exact source hypotheses were also compared. Identity/composition checks use actual model pullback or Deligne norm, not an arbitrary map assumed to have its conclusion.

| Statement | Instances tried | Values computed on the two sides | Result and change |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Green sign and factor two | Spec ℤ, c=1,2; f=2 | degrees 1,2; parent Green values 2,4; principal degree log2−log2=0 | Retained −log norm and exp(−g). |
| Arithmetic absolute normalization | K quadratic, then extension degree 3 | raw degrees 2→6; heights 2/2=6/6=1 | Field degree retained. |
| Geometric base change | k(t)⊂k(u), t=u² | h([1:t])=1, h([1:u²])=2 | Distinguished base change from point representation. |
| Faltings/Deligne metric | g=1,2 | squared norm ratio π⁻¹,π⁻²; degree shifts (logπ)/2,logπ | Retained squared-norm factor 1/2. |
| Twice-theta dual and multiplication | elliptic fibre, m=0,1,2 | degrees 2 versus −2 for the inverse convention; heights 0,a,4a | Dual and rigidification retained. |
| Residue characteristic and level | p=2,3 | choose N=3,4 respectively; gcd(N,p)=1 in both cases | No good-prime restriction. |
| Local norm rescaling | loop length 1, unramified or ramified quadratic extension | fixed/unramified length 1, ramified normalized length 2; τ=1/12 or 1/6 | Uses e, not field/residue degree alone. |
| Empty and zero inputs | Spec zero ring; empty power index; zero vector space | Spec0=∅ violates integral-variety hypotheses; degree 2g−2 is −2,0,2 for g=0,1,2 | No junk arithmetic degree or 0/0 assigned; pair scope g>1. |
| Square-zero fibre | x²=2 mod2 | fibre 𝔽₂[x]/(x²), reduced test 𝔽₂ | §12.6 repaired. |
| Purely inseparable pullback | Frobenius on the five-point elliptic curve over 𝔽₅ | order-five L≠O but F*L=L⁵=O | §3.4 requires geometrically reduced fibres. |
| Commutative Picard operation | cyclic C₂; attempted nonabelian S₃ | 2·1=0 in C₂; (12)(23)=(123)≠(132)=(23)(12) | Picard symmetry excludes S₃ as a model; no general injectivity of multiplication is asserted. |
| Zero dimension versus global-field model dimension | Spec finite k-extension; Spec number field over ℤ | geometric h⁰=extension degree>0; arithmetic O(0) has volume 0 in absolute dimension 1 | Conventions retain model dimension. |
| Singular and smooth metric input | genus-two banana versus smooth reduction | atomic δv₁+δv₂ versus edge density (2/3)Σdx; both masses 2 | Equal mass cannot replace curvature equality. |
| Coercive divisor | unit K₄ | τ=5/16, ε=11/6, φ=17/48; D−Δ=−181/48, D−cΔ=15/8 | Stronger coefficient rejected. |
| Siu zero denominator | A=O(2), B=O(0) | mixed degree 0, volume 2 for all ε>0 | Separate zero case. |
| Potential bigness index | d=0, e=1, m=1 versus m=0 | curve degree a=2 versus empty exterior sum 0 | Criterion uses m≥1. |
| Relative-power exponent selection | e=1,m=2 | (2,1),(1,2) contribute aM; (3,0),(0,3) contribute 0 | Fibre-dimension vanishing is applied before exponent counting. |
| Torsor/base metric normalization | two extensions differing by O(1) | ratio e⁻¹; adjunction/mean conditions force twist 0 for the canonical pair | Ordinary admissibility and the canonical pair are distinguished. |

| 1.8 Adelic line bundles on an arithmetic curve | For K=ℚ, O_ℚ×/{±1}=0 gives compact degree-zero Picard group 0 | For K=ℚ, O_ℚ×/{±1}=0 gives compact degree-zero Picard group 0; removing 2 gives one ℝ/ℤ coordinate; adjoining all primes gives ⊕ₚℝ/ℤ. | Consistent with the stated hypotheses and source |
| 2.1 Global Berkovich analytification | Spec k has one analytic point | Spec k has one analytic point; the ℤ archimedean ray has ∣2∣=2^t, giving 2 at t=1 and √2 at t=1/2, while the 2-adic point has ∣2∣=1/2. | Unbounded scheme-coordinate radii retained |
| 2.2 Analytic arithmetic divisors and continuous metrized line bundles | For D=div(2), g∞=−log 2 and exp(−g∞)=2 | For D=div(2), g∞=−log 2 and exp(−g∞)=2; using exp(−g∞/2) would give √2. | 2.3 Analytification of adelic divisors and line bundles is injective and fully faithful | O(1) and O(2) on Spec ℤ have Green values 1 and 2 at the ordinary archimedean point and 0 on each p-adic ray, so the analytic realization separates them. | O(1) and O(2) on Spec ℤ have Green values 1 and 2 at the ordinary archimedean point and 0 on each p-adic ray, so the analytic realization separates them. | Consistent with the stated hypotheses and source |
| 2.4 Density of divisorial points | For k(t), ord₀ gives ∣t∣=e⁻¹ and ord∞ gives ∣t∣=e | For k(t), ord₀ gives ∣t∣=e⁻¹ and ord∞ gives ∣t∣=e; rescaling ord₀ by a→0 gives ∣t∣→1; Spec k has only the value 1 on k×. | Consistent with the stated hypotheses and source |

Nonzero P=(1,1) has order 5; F*O(P−O)=O(5P−5O)=O, so geometrically nonreduced Frobenius fibres defeat integral injectivity. | Consistent with the stated hypotheses and source |

| 4.5 Bigness of nef bundles under finite covers | For a finite degree-two cover, a nef top intersection 3 pulls back to 6 | For a finite degree-two cover, a nef top intersection 3 pulls back to 6; positivity is equivalent in both directions, whereas 0 pulls back to 0. | Consistent with the stated hypotheses and source |
| 4.6 Domination by nef effective model divisors | On Spec ℤ, O(−1)≤O(0)≤O(1), so a nef effective upper bound exists | On Spec ℤ, O(−1)≤O(0)≤O(1), so a nef effective upper bound exists; replacing the upper bound by O(−2) fails −1≤−2. | Consistent with the stated hypotheses and source |

## Adversarial pass: Checks and worked examples

Each design Check is also evaluated against the formulas of its subsection. Repeated computations in the reader's layer examples and final worked examples use the same normalization; they are not alternative definitions. The added explicit witnesses appear in the target table above. The source plan's Checks are individually indexed here.

For the independent K₄ calculation, each of three edges incident to a vertex contributes ∫₀¹(t−t²/2)dt=1/3 to its resistance integral, and each of three opposite edges contributes ∫₀¹(1/2+t(1−t)/2)dt=7/12. Since μₐ has density 1/6 on every edge, ε=4·(3/3+21/12)/6=11/6. The resistance-derivative integrals give τ=(3·1/3+3·1/12)/4=5/16; the polarized-graph identity yields φ=17/48. Thus the negative-control value D−Δ is computed independently, rather than inferred from a weaker proved inequality. The exact rational constants A(4)=139, A(2)=429/4 and the g≥5 bound 877/8 were recomputed, as were theta cancellation at g=2,3,4 and the finite elliptic points used for Frobenius.

No unrestricted noncompactness, non-Hausdorffness, non-abelian group law, inseparable section field or nonreduced total-space hypothesis was silently added to a headline. Integral finite-type bases, complete local valued fields and positive-degree denominators are explicitly part of the corresponding statements. The singular/pluripotential and unavailable analytic supplier interfaces remain declared gaps rather than unproved local definitions.

## Final structural verification

The final reader has 14 layer headings, 14 Examples endings, 14 Dependencies endings and 90 retained target subsections. Every retained definition/construction has at least three Checks. All three supplier-gap sentences are literal matches of their plan entries. The source manifest has 20 unique, complete keys. The only change to design/plan.json is the gaps value; the surrounding bytes match the tracked original. All README code spans that name mathematical declarations refer to pinned suppliers; the package declares none. No machine paths occur in the deliverables.
